I don't really know if I'd call it guilt. I just... I don't feel great about it. It's... I guess it's hard to explain. Once I start on those nights... I don't know, it's almost like I'm watching myself from the outside. I'll be in my head telling myself to just put the food away, but... my hands just keep opening containers. I just keep going until everything is gone.\n\nDoctor: How often do these late-night eating episodes happen?\n\nPatient: Um... probably at least once a week. Or... sometimes I'll throw up. Just to... try and fix it, I guess.\n\nDoctor: How often do you throw up after one of these eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Um... well, like I was just saying, since the apartment move settled into a routine, it's most weeks that I have at least one of those late nights... where I eat way past what I meant to. And... throwing up is just one of the ways I try to make up for it. \n\nI don't do it every single time. Sometimes I just skip breakfast or... force myself on that long run instead. So... I just... eat.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel stressed, anxious, or upset before these late-night eating episodes happen?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really know. I don't... pay much attention to how I'm feeling right before. Like I said, it just kind of happens.\n\nDoctor: Are you afraid of gaining weight?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. I guess I am. I just... I get really panicky if my pants feel even a little tighter, or if the scale goes up just a pound. Even when people at the venue tell me I look too lean, I just... I worry that the guests or my coworkers will notice right away and think I let myself go.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you would still eat this way if you didn't worry about gaining weight?\n\nPatient: I don't know. I haven't really... thought about it like that. I guess it's hard to imagine not worrying about it.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Just to... try and fix it, I guess.\n\nDoctor: How often do you throw up after one of these eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Um... well, like I was just saying, since the apartment move settled into a routine, it's most weeks that I have at least one of those late nights... where I eat way past what I meant to. And... throwing up is just one of the ways I try to make up for it. \n\nI don't do it every single time. Sometimes I just skip breakfast or... force myself on that long run instead. So... I don't know exactly how often for that specifically. It just depends.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel hungry when these late-night eating episodes start?\n\nPatient: Um... not always. Sometimes I am, I guess. But... mostly I don't really pay attention to that. I just... eat.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel stressed, anxious, or upset before these late-night eating episodes happen?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really know. I don't... pay much attention to how I'm feeling right before. Like I said, it just kind of happens.\n\nDoctor: Are you afraid of gaining weight?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. I guess I am. I just... I get really panicky if my pants feel even a little tighter, or if the scale goes up just a pound. Even when people at the venue tell me I look too lean, I just... I worry that the guests or my coworkers will notice right away and think I let myself go.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you would still eat this way if you didn't worry about gaining weight?\n\nPatient: I don't know. I haven't really... thought about it like that. I guess it's hard to imagine not worrying about it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your late-night eating episodes would happen less often if you weren't worried about gaining weight?\n\nPatient: I really couldn't say. Um... like I just said, I haven't really thought about it. Maybe? I just... I don't know if it would change anything.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're in control of what you're eating during those late-night episodes?\n\nPatient: No, not really. It's... um. Once I start eating on those nights, it's like I'm not even the one doing it. I'm just sort of... watching myself from the outside. I keep telling myself to stop, to just put the food away, but... my hands just keep opening containers. I just keep going until it's all gone.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your desire to eat late at night would go away if you weren't worried about your weight?\n\nPatient: I... look, like I said a minute ago, I really don't know. You keep asking about the weight thing, but I just... I don't have an answer for that. I can't really picture it.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually throw up after these episodes because you feel uncomfortable or because you're trying to undo the calories you consumed?\n\nPatient: Um... I guess it's the calories. Like, if I get home from work and eat too much takeout or snacks, I just... I go to the bathroom and make myself throw up. I just don't want it to sit in me and... turn into weight.\n\nDoctor: Is throwing up after eating mostly about undoing the calories you consumed or is it about feeling uncomfortable after eating?\n\nPatient: I... didn't I just answer that? Like I said, it's... it's the calories. I just... yeah. That's pretty much it.\n\nDoctor: Is your main goal when throwing up after eating to get rid of the calories you just consumed?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I mean, I thought I just said that. If I eat a bunch of takeout or whatever after work, I just... I make myself throw up so I don't gain weight from it. That's the main reason.\n\nDoctor: Is getting rid of the calories the only reason you throw up after eating, or are there other reasons as well?\n\nPatient: I... I really don't know what else you want me to say. Like I've been saying, it's just about the weight. I get home, I eat too much of whatever, and I go to the bathroom to make myself throw up so it doesn't sit there. There's no... other reason. That's just what I do.\n\nDoctor: How many times a month do you throw up after eating?\n\nPatient: I don't know the exact number... I guess since the apartment move settled into a routine, most weeks I have at least one late-night episode. I just eat way past what I meant to, and then... I try to make up for it. Sometimes by throwing up, or skipping breakfast the next day, or forcing a long run. So, maybe a few times a month for the throwing up part.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
